USC-Autorisation

# (OK until)

BEFORE PROCEEDING IMPORTANT: A client can appoint only one representative for each application they submit. If you have not been trained to add authorization forms in GCMS:

- Follow this procedure.
For Certificate of Identity (COI) or Refugee Travel Document (RTD) applications: If you have been trained on this line of business (LOB): o follow this procedure; If not: 0change the title of the email to Certificate of Identity/Refugee Travel Document; and 0 transfer the email to the Question box. If the enquiry contains questions on a LOB that you have been trained on : You must respond to the other questions instead of transferring the email to the Question box. If a representative was designated through the eTA online application tool: No IMM 5475 or IMM 5476 is necessary to assist them should they ask questions during the processing of the application;

000006

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomn

The authorization box that was checked during the submission of the application is sufficient, as long as the representative is mentioned in the Notes view of the eTA application.

#### If the requester is providing several documents/IMM 5475 or 5476 in a webform, you can

attach them only if they are:

- All for the same client; or For different clients who share the same file.

In the above case:

Attach the documents as per this procedure; and If the only question was to add documents, reply to requester using the text Documents attached.

#### If the requester is providing documents/IMM 5475 or 5476 for different clients who do

not share the same file:

Reply to client using the text Documents provided for more than one client.

If an individual was authorized on an application that was cancelled due to technical issues/an administrative error and transferred to a new number:

The third party is also authorized on the new application, unless indicated otherwise.

If an authorization was cancelled due to the application being linked to an online account:

### You can accept the original authorization form as this is due to a system limitation.

If you are following any of the procedures below and are unable to enter a note in the Notes view of the application:

You may leave the note in the Notes view of the Client screen instead.

# Extérieur du Canada

Si l'autorisation est au dossier, mais n'est pas entrée correctement au système (p.ex., formulaire est dans la vue Correspondance > Reçue, mais n'est pas dans la vue Clients & parties, etc.) :

- Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte Veuillez consulter le bureau des visas.
Pour une demande initialement soumise ou présentement en traitement à l'extérieur du Canada, vérifiez :

- Si le RÉP désigne/annuleun représentant/personne désignée : si le formulaire est complet :
suivez la procédure Ajouter IMM 5475-ou 5476-appropriée(c.-à-d., Ajouter IMM 5475, Ajouter IMM 5476 ou Annuler l'autorisation de communiquer ou Recours aux services d'un représentant).

# si le formulaire est incomplet :

répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476 - Incomplet.

- Si le DP et/ou DEP désigne/annuleun représentant rémunéré canadienqui est un membre en règle ou un représentant non rémunéréou une personne désignée : si le formulaire est complet :
000017

Immigration, Réfugiés Immigration, Refugees

# 1*1and Citizenship Canada et Citoyenneté Canada

Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l’accès à linformation

■ répondez au représentant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476 - Demande traitée à l'extérieur du Canada; si le formulaire est incomplet : répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476 - Incomplet-Demande traitée à l'extérieur du Canada.

- Si le représentant n'est PAS un membre en règle : o suivez cette procédure.
Pour une demande fermée à l'extérieur du Canada :

- Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte Autorisation-RI-Demande fermée.

## (OK until)

### IMM 5475/5476

Ensure the IMM 5475 or IMM 5476 is complete by confirming:

Clarifications

# To verify

All fields are All fields in the corresponding sections (appointing a representative completed, unless or cancelling the appointment of a representative) are complete. the question indicates ifWhen there is a small discrepancy, if there is reasonable applicableevidence that the information is correct, you can accept it: examples that are acceptable (this list is not exhaustive): the name of the representative on the form is Mike, but the member's name on the designated organization's website is Michael; the name of the representative on the form is Kevin, but the member's name on the designated organization's website is Shan and their official email address is shan.kevin@email.com; the address is slightly incomplete, but you are able to confirm it by doing a search and there is no doubt concerning its validity (e.g., there is only one search result); the address contains the name of the city instead of the suburb (e.g., Toronto vs. North York). The form is incomplete if the information differs significantly and there is no reasonable evidence to support it:

000035

Students-at-law

TheyStudents-at-lawcan be representatives even if they are not members in good standing of a Canadian provincial or territorial law society if they meet certain criteria.

Compensated/Uncompensated Then...Students-at-law-can-be-authorized

### If student-at-law is... i#f Uneompensated CompensatedTheycan be authorized if theyare under the supervision of:

Lawyers and paralegals who are members in good standing of a Canadian provincial or territorial law society; or Notaries who are members in good standing of the Chambre des notaires du Québec.

The member in good standing and/or the student-at-law must provide the IMM 5476 along with a satisfactory explanation, at your discretion, so you can determine with certainty that the student is under the supervision of the member.

Uncompensated Theycan be authorized if theyare under the supervision of consultants, lawyers or Quebec notaries in good standing who do not charge a fee or receive compensation.

The member in good standing and/or the student-at-law must provide the IMM 5476 along with a satisfactory explanation, at your discretion, so you can determine with certainty that the student is under the supervision of the member.

Note: The previous version of the IMM 5476 (August 2020) is accepted until April 30, 2021. Effective

May 1, 2021, any new IMM 5476 received must be the current version (February 2021). Any new IMM 5475 received must be the current version (August 2020). Any new IMM 5475 received must be the current version (August 2020).

### IMM 5476 form:

The IMM 5476 form was updated in July 2021 in both English and French. During this period of transition, the February 2021 and April 2021 versions of the form will be acceptable until September 30, 2021. Starting October 1, 2021, any new IMM 5476 received must be the current version from July

2021. Any previous versions of the IMM 5476 will no longer be acceptable as of that date. IMM 5475 form:

### Formulaire IMM 5476:

Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en juillet 2021 en anglais et en français. Durant cette période de transition, les versions de février 2021 et d'avril 2021 du formulaire seront acceptées jusqu'au 30 septembre 2021. À partir du 1er octobre 2021, tout nouvel IMM 5476 reçu doit être la version actuelle de juillet

## 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date.

### Formulaire IMM 5475 :

Tout nouvel IMM 5475 reçu doit être la version actuelle: oaoût 2020 en anglais; ou mai 2021 en français.

Si vous recevez une version antérieure du formulaire IMM 5475/5476, alors :

- Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476 - Refusé-Version antérieure du formulaire fournie; et Ajoutez une note : CSC a reçu formulaire (IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476) neemplet par (courriel OU télécopieur). Requête refusée car une version antérieure périmée du formulaire a été fournie. Courriel envoyé au (client OU représentant) à (entrez adresse de courriel) pour aviser d'envoyer un nouveau formulaire. 2021. Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date. The IMM 5476 form was updated in July 2021 in both English and French. During this period of transition, the February 2021 and April 2021 versions of the form will be acceptable until September 30, 2021. Starting Oeteber 1, 2021, any new IMM 5476 received must be the current version from July
2021. Any previous versions of the IMM 5476 will no longer be acceptable as of that date.

IMM 54765475/5476 - Version antérieure du formulaire Formulaire IMM 5475 : Tout nouvel IMM 5475 reçu doit être la version actuelle de mai 2021 en anglais et en français. Les versions antérieures ne sont plus acceptées. Formulaire IMM 5476 : Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en octobre 2021 en anglais et en français.

### Durant cette période de transition, la version précédente de juillet 2021 sera acceptée jusqu’au 30

décembre 2021. À partir du 1 janvier 2022, tout nouvel IMM 5476 reçu doit être la version actuelle d'octobre 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date. Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en juillet 2021 en anglais et en français. Durant cette période de transition, les versions de février 2021 et d'avril 2021 du formulaire seront acceptées jusqu'au 30 septembre 2021. À partir du 1er octobre 2021, tout nouvel IMM 5476 reçu doit être la version actuelle de juillet

2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date.

IMM 5475/5476 - Version antérieure du formulaire

#### Formulaire IMM 5475 :

Tout nouvel IMM 5475 reçu doit être la version actuelle de mai 2021mai 2021en anglais et en français. Les versions antérieures ne sont plus acceptées.

#### Formulaire IMM 5476 :

Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en ectobre 2021octobre 2021en anglais et en français.

Durant cette période de transition, la version précédente de juillet 2021 sera acceptée jusqu'au 30 décembre 2021.

Bien qu'il soit encouragé d'utiliser la version la plus récente du formulaire IMM 5476, la version précédente (juillet 2021) sera acceptée jusqu'au 31 décembre 2021.

À partir du 1 janvier 20221 janvier 2022, tout nouvel IMM 5476 reçu doit être la version actuelle d’octobre 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date.

Si vous recevez une version antérieure du formulaire IMM 5475/5476, alors :

Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476Refusé Version antérieure du formulaire fournie; et Ajoutez une note :

CSC a reçu formulaire (IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476) par (courriel OU télécopieur). Requête refusée car une version antérieure du formulaire a été fournie. Courriel envoyé au (client Ou représentant) à (entrez adresse de courriel) pour aviser d'envoyer un nouveau formulaire. INIT-ID

### For the paid representative, verify:

- If they are in good standing* (also available in IP9);
if the regulatory body's website indicates that the representative has a restrictive permit, follow this procedure; ICCRC-College of Immigration and Citizenship Consultants (CICC) representatives can practice both in Canada and outside Canada;

# if the representative has an address outside Canada, add their address in the note

only; a representative from a lawyer's office, other than the lawyer indicated in the application can call for info only; in the note to be added on file, articling students (students at law) are to be identified by name along with their supervising lawyer's contact information and ID, and the firm's contact details; only Ontario's law society, the Law Society of Upper Canada, admits paralegals as members in good standing: if a paralegal is not a member in good standing of a law society, or is not a member in good standing of the ICCRCCICC, then they are not an authorized representative;

- for CIT applications: Othe above information applies to applications received after June 11, 2015,
only.

# (OK until)

FURTHER INFO

Requests for Case Specific Information Call Centre Operational Guidelines-FAQ IP9 Use of a Family Member Representative for Online Applications (IMM 5713) Othis form can only be added via an online application. Operational Bulletin 670

# (OK until)

### Pour le représentant rémunéré, vérifiez :

- S'il est un membre en règle* (aussi disponible dans IP9); si le site Web de l’autorité responsable indique que le représentant rémunéré détient
un permis restrictif, suivez cette procédure;

# les représentants CRCICdu Collège des consultants en immigration et en citoyenneté

(CCIC)peuvent pratiquer à la fois au Canada et à l'extérieur du Canada; si le représentant a une adresse à l'extérieur du Canada, ajoutez son adresse dans la note seulement; un représentant d'un cabinet d'avocats peut s'informer sur une demande détenue par le cabinet, mais ne peut pas prendre les mesures requises; dans la note à ajouter dans la demande, les étudiants inscrits au barreau (stagiaires en droit) doivent être identifiés à l'aide de leur nom avec les coordonnées, le numéro d'identification de leur avocat superviseur et les coordonnées du cabinet d'avocats; actuellement, seul le barreau de l'Ontario, soit le Barreau du Haut-Canada, admet les techniciens juridiques en règle; si un technicien juridique n'est pas un membre en règle du barreau ni un membre en règle du CRCICCCIC, il n'est pas un représentant autorisé; pour les demandes CIT :

## cette information s'applique à toutes les demandes reçues après le 11 juin

2015 seulement.

IMM 5475/5476 - Outdatedor previous version of form Previeus-versien

IMM 5475 form:

Any newIMM 5475 formIMM 5475 received must be the current version from May 2021 in English and French. Previous versions are no longer accepted.

IMM 5476 form:

The IMM 5476 form was updated in November 2021 in English and French.

Starting April 1, 2022, any IMM 5476 received for a newly submitted application must be the current version from November 2021, Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date for any newly submitted application.

Although using the current version of the IMM 5476 form is encouraged, some previous versions may be accepted for applications submitted before March 31, 2022:

- The previous versíon from October 2021 is acceptable until March 31, 2022; The prior version from July 2021 is acceptable until December 31, 2021; any other previous version is outdated and must not be accepted,
Note:

- If a representative Party ID line is cancelled and indicates No longer Representative in the Disassoc Reason field in GCMS due to the representative linking the application to their online account, the procedures in this section are still applicable for adding a representative or designated individual as long as it is for the same person indicated in the Party ID line and all the information matches.

The IMM 5476 form was updated in October 2021 in both English and French.

Although accepting the most updated version of the IMM 5476 form is encouraged, the previous version (July 2021) will be accepted until December 31, 2021.

Starting January 1, 2022, any new IMM 5476 received must be the current version from October

2021. Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date.

### IMM 5475/5476 - Version antérieure ou précédente du formulaire

#### Formulaire IMM 5475 :

000179

Toutnouveau formulaire IMM 5475nouvel IMM 5475reçu doit être la version actuelle de mai 2021 en anglais et en français. Les versions antérieures ne sont plus acceptées. Formulaire IMM 5476 : Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en novembre 2021 en anglais et en français. A compter du 1 avril 2022, tout IMM 5476 reçu pour une demande nouvellement soumise doit être la version actuelle de novembre 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date pour toute nouvelle demande présentée. Bien qu'll soit encouragé d'utiliser la version actuelle du formulaire IMM 5476, certaines versions

### antérieures peuvent être acceptées pour les demandes soumises avant le 31 mars 2022 :

La version précédente d'octobre 2021 est acceptable jusqu'au 31 mars 2022;

### La version d’avant de juillet 2021 est acceptable jusqu'au 31 décembre 2021;

toute autre version antérieure est périmée et ne doit pas être acceptée.

Note:

Partie et que toutes les informations correspondent. Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en octobre 2021 en anglais et en français.

précédente (juillet 2021) sera acceptée jusqu'au 31 décembre 2021.

Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date.

De : CE CSC Procedures / Procédures CSC EC (IRCC) <IRCC.CECSCProcedures-ProceduresCSCEC.IRCC@cic.gc.ca> Envoyé : 4 avril 2022 16:15 À : Lapointe.Annik <Annik.Lapointe@cic.gc.ca> Cc : CE CSC Procedures / Procédures CSC EC (IRCC) <IRCC.CECSCProcedures-ProceduresCSCEC.IRCC@cic.gc.ca> Objet : RE: CIAD-2022-3991 ESU-UOR-Authorization-Updated IMM 5476 form (November 2021)

Salut Annik, Merci de nous revenir sur cecì, on a fait les mods et répondu à tes questions ©

Il va falloir garder les mentions de juillet et octobre 2021 malheureusement (voir mes commentaires dans le doc pour plus d’infos). And moving forward, we'll have to adjust the dates as we did now if there are upcoming form releases.

Ce n’est pas une question de l’inventaire ESU en tant que tel, mais c’est plutôt la façon dont les agents UOR évaluent les formulaires IMM 5476 dépendamment de la version, la date de signature sur le formulaire vs la date de réception de la demande. En se basant sur ces points les agents peuvent déterminer si le formulaire est périmé ou non (i.e., authorization is on file but not properly added and an older form version is on file or authorization is on file but not properly added and no form is on file). This was established by CSGS (Linda) moving forward for the pre-July 2021 form versions so the same logic applies to the July and October 2021 versions for the mentioned time periods.

Also keep in mind that that there are open/ongoing applications in GCMS that may or may not even be entered in the system yet from before July 1, 2021 (i.e., TRPR apps which are not all entered in GCMS, same goes for PR Online apps, etc.) so we would still need to keep this for agents to determine if the form is acceptable based on the time period it was signed/submitted vs the application reception date.

J’espère que ça clarifie, je sais que c’est ben compliqué mais on arrive!

Laisse-moi savoir s’il y a de quoi et je vais l’envoyer pour affichage par la suite.

Merci!

Allô Annik, Ce que IRCU m’ont expliqué c’est que ces 5 mois ne sont pas fixes. C'est pour ça qu'ils s’engagent de nous laisser savoir chaque fois qu'il y a un changement avec des instructions spécifiques. Je comprends tout à fait que l'on veut éviter de faire sans cesse des changements aux procédures et que ce soit le plus efficace possible pour les agents mais de dire d’accepter la dernière version pendant 5 mois peut comporter aussi des erreurs. Aussi, je ne crois pas qu'il y ait des changements tous les mois comme c’est arrivé. Octobre, c'était pour des problèmes de codage apparemment et novembre pour le nouveau collège. Est-ce que ça aiderait de mettre le lien de notre site Use of a Representative Form? Je vois que dans la procédure les agents vérifient déjà si le consultant est règle. Est-ce que tu crois que ça peut aider les agents ?

## IMM 5476 form:

The latest version of the IMM 5476 form can be found here was updated in October 2021-November in both English and French.

Although accepting the most updated version of the IMM 5476 form is encouraged if previous versions are in the inventory they must still be accepted:

000196

Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canada et Citoyenneté Canada

s.16(2)(c) Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act
L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l'accès à l'information

- the-previous version July 2021 will be accepted until December 31, 2021. October 2021 will be accepted until March 31st, 2022.
Starting April 1st, 2022, any new IMM 5476 received must be the current version from October November 2021. Main LOB Use of Representative enquiries (IMM 5476) (or general, ATIP, fraud) Question/issue We have received an enquiry from ESU regarding the most recent version of the IMM 5476 summarized: form.

When we last communicated about the IMM 5476 (CIAD-2021-3596, see attached), the most recent version published was 10/2021. However, it appears that a new version was created in 11/2021.

The ESU procedure for outdated forms, found here, currently refers to the version from 10/2021. Therefore, we would require some clarification regarding the acceptance of the now previous 10/2021 form since a newer version was created in 11/2021.

In the correspondence attached, there is anotethat mentions the following: Although accepting the most updated version of the form is encouraged (10/2021), the previous version of the IMM5476 (07/2021) can be accepted up to 90 days following the release of the October 2021 form.

Since a new form was release in 11/2021, should we be applying the same logic as follows:

The 07/2021 form would be accepted until December 31, 2021 (90 days following the 10/2021 release); The 10/2021 form would be accepted until January 31, 2022 (90 days following the 11/2021 release); and The 11/2021 form should be the only form accepted from February 1, 2022 onwards?

Can you confirm if this would be the correct way to proceed when it comes to accepting previous versions of the IMM 5476 form?

Or can the agents continue to accept both the 10/2021 and 11/2021 forms for efficiency and to avoid unnecessary back and forth between client/rep? Context/additional N/A information:

in GCMS?

If yes to the above, is there any indication in GCMS that the rep has been cancelled or disassociated?

As soon as possible Response due date, if any: Main LOB Use of Representative enquiries (IMM 5475/5476) (or general, ATIP, fraud) Question/issue Good day CSGS, summarized: We have received an enquiry from ESU regarding additional guidance for accepting outdated IMM 5475 / IMM 5476 forms (as you know the version have been updated quite a few times over the past year or so).

Here is a timeline of all the updates until this day that I have managed to find and have cross-checked with ESU as well:

1. IMM 5475/5476 (03/2014 version) → no longer being accepted as of 08/2019
2. New IMM 5475 version created in 06/2019
Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canada et Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l'accès à l'information

000209

3. New IMM 5476 version created in 08/2019
4. New IMM 5475 / 5476 version created in 08/2020
a. 5475 (06/2019) & 5476 (08/2019) accepted until 2020/11/30
5. New IMM 5476 version created on 02/2021 & 04/2021 (FR only based on background attached)
a. both versions (02 and 04 2021) accepted until Šeptember 30, 2021
b. the previous 5476 version from 08/2020 was acceptable until April 30, 2021 (see 6.b for comment on IMM 5475)
6. New IMM 5476 version created on 07/2021
a. acceptable from July 2021 - December 31, 2021
b. For the IMM 5475, in July, I noticed that the 5475 in FR only was updated in 05/21 but the EN version was still dated 08/20 back then however this now seems to have been resolved and both 5475 forms in EN/FR show 05/21 as the most up to date version of the 5475 (so between December 1, 2020 and April 30, 2021 this means that the 08/2020 version of the 5475 was still acceptable, please correct me if I'm wrong).
7. New IMM 5476 version created on 10/2021
a. acceptable as of October 2021 – until further notice
b. as of January 1, 2022 the 10/2021 is the only acceptable form
c. For the IMM 5475 (see comment 6.b only version now available is from 05/21).
The current ESU procedure for outdated forms can be found here as per the most recent update to the forms in October 2021.

After having done some research in the background over the past few years, I have found the following useful information (see second attachment with the following confirmation from CSGS):

# NOTE: Earlier versions of these forms that were previously submitted in support of an application

remain valid until a new request is received to cancel the representative/ designation, appoint a new representative/ designated person, or update contact information for the representative/designated person. Such amendment requests require that a new form be completed and submitted.

I will integrate this to the above-mentioned section of the ESU procedure as it is not mentioned anywhere and it is extremely relevant for the agents processing these.

However, the problem arises in the following situation:

If a form that was submitted with the application appears in GCMS (can be visualized in the incoming correspondence section of GCMS) and the authorization has been entered by the responsible office when it should not have been (i.e.,due to erroneous validation by office or if the rep appears on file under notes/party ID but the office does not specify who the person is authorized for or if the office does not upload the form to GCMS (form cannot be visualized in

Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canada et Citoyenneté Canada to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la l

000210

GCMS) which prevents the CSC from determining who the rep is authorized for/taking appropriate action), normally the CSC will ask the enquirer to submit a new form by responding with either: OIMM 5475/5476 - Refused-Error by office-Must resubmit form (not on file) O IMM 5475/5476 - Refused-Error by office-Must resubmit form

Currently all our texts, lead to the website which has the most up to date version of the IMM 5475 / 5476 forms. However, from my own experience, reps will try themselves and they will write back to us by using previous versions of these forms regardless.

## So here are my questions:

In theseabove mentioned situations, does the CSC need to go back in time and verify: Owhen the application was submitted? Ovalidate what version of the form was “acceptable” during the time when the application was submitted? (ex. let's say a client submits an application today and the version of the form on file appears to be an older version say from 08/2020 which is no longer acceptable and the form happens to be deemed incomplete for X reason. If an agent accesses the file and encounters any of these situations, when the client resubmits the new form can the CSC accept a prior version should they submit one? Given that the form is from 08/2020 in this case, offices can no longer be accept them anyways (meaning error by office as in the situationsabove), therefore should the CSC make the call to determine whether the form is outdated or not in this case even if the new form is properly filled out this time? Is it possible to confirm if this is still valid for cases where it is for an existing rep that is on file (but has been erroneously entered by the office given these possible scenarios)? My recommendation: If the rep needs to resubmit the form, it should be solely because of the fact that it was deemed incomplete or inthese scenarios. It would be much easier to avoid penalizing the reps inthesecases only in order to avoid an unnecessary back and forth instead of simply refusing it based on the fact that the form is an outdated version. Can you please confirm? If ESU does need to base themselves on the above timeline, we would need clarification as to how Reg Ops would proceed as well because they don't analyze or validate the IMM 5475/IMM 5476? The only current guidelines for Reg Ops are here (leads to ESU for validation).

As you can see it is now starting to get pretty confusing as to what agents need to accept in terms of outdated forms when reps/clients write back to us after having asked them to resubmit the form.

Please advise how ESU should proceed because the vast majority of the ESU Use of Rep enquiries are exactly related to these cases where the office erroneously enters an authorization or does not upload the form which prevents/delays the CSC from taking action accordingly.

Context/additional See background attached from prior years with timeline of the IMM 5475/5476 version updates. information:

## Thank you!

Demandeur incapable d’agir légalement à son propre nom en raison d’un handicap

Si vous recevez une requête par formulaire Web où un parent/tuteur légal veut agir au nom d'un demandeur ayant un handicap (p.ex., il indique que le demandeur est incapable de lire, écrire, parler, signer des documents ou prendre leur propre décisions), une procuration peut être utilisée dans un tel cas.

# Si...Alors...

Aucune demande a été envoyée Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte Procuration-Informations générales. oU

La demande a été envoyée et aucune documentation fournie par le formulaire Web/lorsque la demande a été soumise ou l'autorisation de procuration n'apparaît pas au dossier

Hi Sophie! As discussed yesterday, I have now asked Webcart to remove the mention of this line in the table for applications submitted before March 31, 2022:

- Verify the acceptable form version cut-off dates in this section;
I can confirm to you that the internal UOR table used for keeping track of the cut-off dates is no longer required for the agents.

This is applicable to both scenarios where:

and

- Authorization is entered on file but is not properly added and no form appears on file
to compare the date of signature of the form to the application reception date in GCMS by determining if it is stale dated or not.

Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l'accès à l'information

authorized representative or designated individual on file and client/enquirer now wants to appoint a new representative or designated individual). In summary, the main things that agents need to distinguish is:

000235

For applications submitted before March 31, 2022, as long as representation has not changed (meaning the representative named in Q.5 on the form is the same as the representative that is named on the form on file (if there is one) and/or in the Party ID on file (if there is one) and/or in a note left on file by the office (if there is one)), then agents do not need to validate if the form version is acceptable or not.

- Authorization is entered on file but is not properly added and an older version of the IMM 5475 form (prior to May 2021) / IMM 5476 form (prior to November 2021) is on file
In both cases, they simply need to compare the content of the of the form to the one in GCMS (if it is available in GCMS, otherwise they base themselves on the form that is provided by Web form) and they need

Now apart from the signature date, if the content of the forms are also different (assuming there is a form on file and they provide one by Web form), it is up to the agent to evaluate whether the forms are complete and in the event that there are differences then agents should also advise the representative about the discrepancies (hence by following the discrepancy procedure). For applications submitted on or after April 1, 2022, even if representation has changed, agents should be following the on or after April 1, 2022 instructions (hence the third option of the first table: There is an

When was the application submitted? Before March 31, 2022 or after? Has the representation changed or no? O

I hope this clarifies, please don't hesitate to reach out if you have any other questions about this procedure.

## Thank you!

The applicant/rep submitted a complete older version of It is recommended that should you have to go back to the client/REP the form (that is no longer accepted) and no IMM5476 is to obtain the missing IMM5476 (e.g., form not uploaded to GCMS), uploaded in GCMS. and they provide a signed form close to the date of the received date of the application, one could assume that that form was submitted with the application. If this occurs, it is up to CSC to decide if they are satisfied that this form can be taken.

With that being said, here are some factors that can be taken into consideration when assessing this:

1. Has the form been signed close to the received date of the application.
2. Has the rep already been added as the Party for that applicant in GCMS?
If yes to the above, is there any indication in GCMS that the rep has been cancelled or disassociated?

If no, the IMM5476 provided can be accepted, and there is no need for the CSC to decide if that version of the form was the correct version at the time of signature. Client submits an application today and the version of Both of these factors would render the form not acceptable. As such, the form on file appears to be an older version (e.g., it is recommended a completed updated form be provided by the client/rep.

From:CE CSC Procedures / Procédures CSC EC (IRCC) Sent: August 31, 2022 10:06 AM To: Dammous.Maria; CE CSC Quality / Qualité CSC EC (IRCC) Cc: Lapointe.Annik; CE CSC Procedures / Procédures CSC EC (IRCC) Subject: RE: Privacy question received at Procedures-your input please

Follow Up Flag: Done-Case closed-Sent to Webcart. JF Flag Status: Completed

Good morning Maria and Quality,

## Hope you're all doing well!

This is simply to advise that we have requested Webcart to add the changes in the CSC Operational Guidelines: FAQ section. The changes should be live shortly.

Here are the answers and clarifications to the questions below:

@Maria: regarding this part, yes this would be a concern as it would be considered as divulging information to an unauthorized third party as they are not properly entered in GCMS.

(1) Yes-if the application is open and the authorization is on file, then agents can respond with Please contact visa office assuming the third party is authorized and properly entered on file. This is part of the changes added in the FAQ for clarity.
(2) Agreed, the following modifications will be applied as per this procedure: A text modification will be made to the text IMM 5475 or 5476 - Application processed outside Canada to allow agents to divulge the specific visa office when the form is complete. oA paragraph will also be added as a yellow highlighted option in the texts IMM 5475 or 5476 - Error by office-Must resubmit form and IMM 5475 or 5476 - Error by office-Must resubmit form (not on file) to direct client/rep to contact the visa office specific Web Form without divulging the actual visa office (similar to the wording in the IMM 5475 or 5476 - Incomplete-Application processed outside Canada text). This will correspond with the changes in the FAQ. OA slight correction will be applied in this procedure (in the section If the authorization is on file, but it is not entered correctly in the system (e.g., form is in Correspondence > Incoming view but not in the Člients & Parties view, etc.), the text Please contact visa office will be replaced by the two Error by office texts as the agents will have the option to add/remove directives for contacting the visa office.
(3) If the file is closed, agents should not be determining whether the form is complete or not, it should be directed to the visa office regardless. This approach is also in line with the CSC's UOR mandate as agents should not be adding/removing representatives on IN files but rather directing them to the appropriate channel (being the visa office specific Web form for their area). The rationale behind this is because the client/rep will most likely be attaching the form regarding some information or action that would most likely be concerning IN anyways, meaning the enquiry itself should ultimately be directed to IN. This concern has also previously been discussed in the past with management and it has been confirmed that the CSC should not be taking on these cases as it falls under IN mandate and they have the budget and staffing to take on these cases. Also the website indicates: Use this page if you live outside of Canada and are instructed to contact the visa office for your area. Client does not necessarily have to choose the actual visa office that processed the application, it can be the one for their area whereby it can be redirected to the appropriate IN office (if needed) based on internal Web Form routing rules.
000256

Procédures a reçu la requête ci-dessous en lien avec Privacy et divulguer le bureau des visas qui détient/traite la demande.

Je me permets de vous envoyer les changements proposés par Procédures dans la page Privacy FAQ comme Palmina l’a déjà fait dans le passé pour avoir votre input (à la fois du CSC et de WebCart). Notez qu’on a appliqué les modifications pour tout type de bureau qui détient le dossier et non seulement les bureaux des visas.

## ***Please attach the client's Web form***

# Please fill out all applicable sections

Suggested action□ Add Clarify □ Modify Remove □ English Language □ French English and French URL to existing WebCart [http://cicwebcart.ci.gc.ca/clientrelation/CRU_useOfRep-eng.aspx#pro7](http://cicwebcart.ci.gc.ca/clientrelation/CRU_useOfRep-eng.aspx#pro7) page Client ID/UCI Appl/Case # Sources consulted □ PSU Team leader □ IRCC website Please provide URL to page/section □ Program Information Please provide URL to page/section □ Connexion Please provide URL to page/section Other (Webcart) [http://cicwebcart.ci.gc.ca/programinfo/tools/privacy_module/qa-eng.aspx#3](http://cicwebcart.ci.gc.ca/programinfo/tools/privacy_module/qa-eng.aspx#3) Details of the request What is your request? Our request is related to the possibility of divulging the visa office holding a file when the authorization on file is complete. We would like to know why this may not be considered a breach:

000259

1) If the application is open and authorization is already on file we should answer with “Please contact visa office”. We assume that this is in case the form is complete, but the procedure does not specify that this is possible only when the form is complete.
2) If the application is open and the client/rep sends a form that is complete, we ask them to send the form to the visa office, but we don't say which one.
3) Whether the form is complete or not, if the file is closed we refer them to the visa office, but we don't say which one.
As per the CHD (Reg. Ops) and the Privacy FAQ, no personal information should be disclosed to an unauthorized third party. If the representative is not properly added and the application is Outside Canada, the fact of letting them know that the file it at XX Visa Office is not considered a privacy breach?

What led you to come up with this request? After evaluating an email we found that there was a privacy breach: 1) Case-specific information was disclosed, 2) the IMM 5476 was only in the IC, and 3) the file was closed and outside Canada.

Despite the fact of having sent the wrong script “Please contact visa office” instead of “Authorization — IR – Closed Application", the TL does not consider it a breach because, if the application had been open, the UOR procedures instruct agents to send the text that was sent by the agent.

Very frequently (i.e., at least once a week) How often do you get emails Frequently (i.e., at least once a month) on this topic? Rarely (i.e., a few times a year) Very rarely (i.e., not more than once per year) Thank you!

## AVANT DE PROCÉDER

# (OK until)

Si le requérant fournit plusieurs documents/IMM 5475 ou 5476 dans le formulaire Web, vous pouvez les joindre seulement s'ils sont:

Tous pour le même client; ou Pour différents clients qui partagent le même dossier.

##### Dans le cas ci-dessus:

Ajoutez le(s) document(s) selon cette procédure; et Si la seule question était d'ajouter le(s) document(s), répondez au client en utilisant le texte Documents joints.

Si un client/requérant fournit des documents/IMM 5475 ou 5476 pour différents elients qui ne partagent pas le même dossier:

Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte Documents fournis pour plus d'un client.

Si le requérant fournit plusieurs documents/IMM 5475/5476/Procuration dans le formulaire Web et ils sont :

- Tous pour le même client ou pour différents clients qui partagent le même dossier : 0ajoutez le(s) document(s) selon cette procédure; o si la seule question était d'ajouter le(s) document(s) :
répondez au client en utilisant le texte Documents joints.

- Pour différents clients qui ne partagent pas le même dossier : répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte Documents fournis pour plus d'un
client.

#### Si une tierce partie était autorisée pour une demande qui a été anntuléeAnnulée dû à

des problèmes techniques/une erreur administrative et transférée à un nouveau numéro :

- La tierce partie est également autorisée pour la nouvelle demande, sauf indication contraire.
Si une autorisation a été annulée et dissociée puisque la demande a été liée à un compte en ligne :

000287

Immigration, Réfugiés 1*and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomn

Vous pouvez accepter le formulaire d’autorisation original puisque ceci est dû à une limitation du système.

Si en-suivant l'une des procédures ci-dessousvous avise d’ajouter une note dans la vue des Notes de la demande et vous n'êtes pas en mesure de l’ajouter ici : d'ajouter une note dans la vue Notes de la demande :

- Vous pouvez l'ajouter dans la vue Notes de l'écran Clients.

Si vous ajoutez, associez ou annulez une autorisation et que le demandeur fournit une empreinte digitale comme signature :

- Suivez cette procédure.

Consultez la page Évaluer un IMM 5475/5476 ou une procuration pour déterminer si l’autorisation est complète.

Si l’autorisation est considérée comme étant complète, ajoutez et associez* les coordonnées du représentant nommé (pour un IMM 5476 ou une procuration seulement) en suivant les aide- mémoires :

Changement d’adresse pour un représentant; Ajout d'un numéro de téléphone pour un représentant; et Changement d'adresse de courriel.

*Exception : Pour un RP existant, n’associez pas l’adresse à la demande de CRP.

Pour les représentants qui sont membres d'un organisme de règlementation:

•Vérifiez s'il est un membre en règle (accessible dans Edge seulement ou sur notre site Web); 。si vous n'êtes pas en mesure de valider le représentant en ligne, contactez l'organisation désignée par téléphone.

- Si le site Web de l’organisme de règlementation indique que le représentant détient un permis restrictif : o suivez cette procédure.
- Les représentants qui sont membres du Collège des consultants en immigration et en citoyenneté (CCIC) peuvent pratiquer à la fois au Canada et à l'extérieur du Canada;
- Il se peut qu’un représentant autorisé ait une adresse qui est à l’extérieur du Canada;
dans tel cas, ajoutez leur adresse dans la note seulement; et n'associez pas leur adresse à la demande.

•Les membres du personnel d'un cabinet d'avocat (autre que l'avocat identifié dans la demande), peuvent appeler pour avoir des renseignements seulement. Veuillez consulter cette section pour plus d'information; Pour les étudiants inscrits au barreau (stagiaires en droit), ils doivent être identifiés à l'aide de leur nom dans la note au dossier ainsi que les coordonnées et le numéro de membre du superviseur et les coordonnées du cabinet d’avocats; • Seul le Barreau de l'Ontario admet les techniciens juridiques en règle;

o si un technicien juridique n'est pas un membre en règle du barreau ou n’est pas un membre en règle du CCIC, il n'est pas un représentant autorisé. Pour les demandes CIT : otoutes les informations ci-dessus s'appliquent seulement aux demandes reçues après le 11 un 2015. Consultez cette page pour vous assurer que l'IMM 5475/5476/la procuration est complet/complète.

Pour le représentant rémunéré, vérifiez:

S'il est un membre en règle* (aussi disponible sur notre site Web); si le site Web de l'autorité responsable indique que le représentant rémunéré détient un permis restrictif, suivez cette procédure; les représentants du Collège des consultants en immigration et en citoyenneté (CCIC)-peuvent pratiquer à la fois au Canada et à l'extérieur du Canada; si le représentant a une adresse à l'extérieur du Canada, ajoutez son adresse dans la note seulement; un représentant d'un cabinet d'avocats peut s'informer sur une demande détenue par le cabinet, mais ne peut pas prendre les mesures requises; dans la note à ajouter dans la demande, les étudiants inscrits au barreau (stagiaires en droit) doivent être identifiés à l'aide de leur nom avec les coordonnées, le numéro d'identification de leur avocat superviseur et les coordonnées du cabinet d'avocats; seul le Barreau de l'Ontario admet les techniciens juridiques en règle; si un technicien juridique n'est pas un membre en règle du barreau ni un membre en règle du CCIC, il n'est pas un représentant autorisé; pour les demandes CIT: cette information s'applique à toutes les demandes reçues après le 11 juin 2015 seulement.

*Note:

Si vous n'êtes pas en mesure de valider le représentant en ligne, communiquez avee l'organisation désignée par téléphone; et Veuillez utiliser Chrome-pour accéder au-site Web du CCIC.

## Cancelling a representative/designated individual

# Important:

Consult this procedure before proceeding with the cancellation of the representative or designated individual; •(For an IMM 5476) If the form is complete, for the contact information of the: o representative to remove on file: ■ expire their contact information in the Clients screen and in the IMM or CIT screen by consulting these job aids; it is possible that the system will not allow you to expire/associate contact information fields in the IMM or CIT screen if these fields are greyed out; if this is the case, expire the information in the Clients screen only; and follow the instructions below. o the applicant (if no new person is appointed on the form, the contact information of the client must be added back on file): tick off the Primary checkbox for the most recent and valid contact information on file for the client (i.e., the one that does not contain a To date if there is one in the Clients screen for the Telephone #(s), Fax #(s) and E-mail(s) fields and in the Addresses view; if you are unable to determine what is the most recent and valid contact information (whether it is for an address, telephone/fax number and/or e-mail address) for the client because: all previous contact information was already expired (i.e., all listed contact information contains a To Date as an expiry); or there are several contact details listed on file that are still valid (i.e., multiple entries that do not contain a To Date as an expiry): follow the appropriate procedure below; and

000300

et Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomn

reply to them using the text IMM 5475 or 5746- Removed or (if applicable) IMM 5475 or 5476 - Cancelled for PA and minor DEPs only. newly appointed person (if applicable, if also appointed on the same form along

# with the cancellation): follow this procedure.

(For an IMM 5475) follow the appropriate procedure below. Verify if the representative or designated individual is appointed for other applications; if they are not: expire their contact information in the Clients screen and, if applicable, in the IMM or CIT screen by consulting these job aids; (for an IMM 5476 only), if the application is Open, Closed or Cancelled: if the contact information fields in the IMM or CIT screens of the application are not greyed out: associate the contact information of the applicant or the newly appointed representative in the IMM or CIT screen of the application by consulting these job aids; if the contact information fields are greyed out in the IMM or CIT screens of the application: associate the contact information for the applicant or the newly appointed representative in the Client screen only by consulting these job aids; if the address for the elient is not available in GCMS: send them the text How to submit a request for a change of contact information.

If a single individual is representing two elients in the Clients & Parties view and you receive a cancellation request for only one of the applicants the individual is representing:

Modify the comment in the Other Relationship Descrip field to remove the applicant who cancelled the representative and press CTRL+S to save the changes in the field; and Add a note in the IMM or CIT screen:

# ***********(Representative/ Designated individual) ***********

CSC received (IMM 5475 or IMM 5476 or POA) for (paid OR unpaid rep OR designated individual) (enter name of designated individual) to be cancelled for (enter type of application) for (SPR and/or PA and/or DEP only). INIT-ID

IMPORTANT: A client can appoint only one representative for each application they submit. If you have not been trained to add authorization forms in GCMS:

For an authorization related to the situation in Afghanistan or Ukraine: Follow this procedure. For Certificate of Identity (COI) or Refugee Travel Document (RTD) applications:

- If you have been trained on this line of business (LOB): 。follow this procedure; If not: Ochange the title of the email to Certificate of Identity/Refugee Travel Document;
and Otransfer the email to the Question box. If a representative was designated through the eTA online application tool: No IMM 5475 or IMM 5476 is necessary to assist them should they ask questions during the processing of the application; The authorization box that was checked during the submission of the application is sufficient, as long as the representative is mentioned in the Notes view of the eTA application.

000327

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l’accès à linformation

Any new IMM 5475 form received must be the current version from May 2021 in English and French. Previous versions are no longer accepted.

##### IMM 5476 form:

The IMM 5476 form was updated in November 2021 in English and French.

Starting April 1, 2022, any IMM 5476 received for a newly submitted application must be the current version from November 2021. Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date for any newly submitted application.

Although using the current version of the IMM 5476 form is encouraged, some previous versions may be accepted for applications submitted before Mareh 31, 2022:

The previous version from Oetober 2021 is acceptable until Mareh 31, 2022; The prior version from July 2021 is acceptable until December 31, 2021; e any other previous version is outdated and must not be accepted.

Si un représentant a été désigné par l’entremise de l’outil de demande en ligne AVE :

Aucun IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476 n'est nécessaire afin de l'aider pour une question se rapportant à un cas spécifique. La boite qui a été cochée lors de la soumission de la demande est suffisante, tant que le représentant est mentionné dans la vue Notes de la demande d'AVE. Aucun IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476 n’est nécessaire afin de l’aider s'il a des questions durant le traitement de la demande; La boîte d’autorisation qui a été cochée lors de la soumission de la demande est

### suffisante, tant que le représentant est mentionné dans la vue Notes de la demande

d'AVE.

IMM 5475/5476 - Version antérieure désuète ou précédente du formulaire

## Formulaire IMM 5475 :

Tout nouveau formulaire IMM 5475 (Pls remove extra spacing)reçu doit être la version actuelle de mai 2021 en anglais et en français. Les versions antérieures ne sont plus acceptées.

## Formulaire IMM 5476 :

Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en novembre 2021 en anglais et en français.

À compter du 1 avril 2022, tout IMM 5476 reçu pour une demande nouvellement soumise doit être la version actuelle de novembre 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date pour toute nouvelle demande présentée.

Bien qu'il soit encouragé d'utiliser la version actuelle du formulaire IMM 5476, certaines versions antérieures peuvent être acceptées pour les demandes soumises avant le 31 mars 2022:

000363

Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l’accès à linformation

La version précédente d'oetobre 2021 est acceptable jusqu'au 31 mars 2022; La version d'avant de juillet 2021 est acceptable jusqu'au 31 décembre 2021; toute autre version antérieure est périmée et ne doit pas être acceptée.

- Il se peut qu'un représentant autorisé ait une adresse qui est à l’extérieur du Canada; dans tel cas, ajoutez son adresse dans la note seulement; et n’associez pas son adresse à la demande. ## For eTA applications:

When a third party submits an eTA application on behalf of the client and they answer Yes to either of the questions Are you applying on behalf of someone? or Are you applying on behalf of a minor?: o they will be prompted to provide information about themselves (see Part 1(A): Parent/Guardian or Representative Details in the ETA Guide); oonce they submit the application, this will create an auto-generated authorization note in GCMS in the IMM/CIT screen > Notes view of the eTA application: the Office column of the note will indicate GCMS-System; and the note will contain the details and contact information for the parent/guardian or representative applying on behalf of the client.

- If there is an auto-generated authorization note created by GCMS-System containing such information: no IMM 5475 or IMM 5476 is necessary to assist them should they ask questions
during the processing of the application; Otherwise, if the auto-generated note does not appear in the Notes view of the application:

Si un représentant a été désigné par l'entremise de l'outil de demande en ligne AVE:

Aucun IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476 n'est nécessaire afin de l'aider s'il a des questions durant le traitement de la demande; La boîte d'autorisation qui a été cochée lors de la soumission de la demande est suffisante, tant que le représentant est mentionné dans la vue Notes de la demande d'AVE.

#### Pour les demandes d'AVE:

Lorsqu'une tierce partie soumet une demande d'AVE au nom du client et il répond Oui à l’une des questions Faites-vous une demande pour une autre personne? ou Faites-vous une demande au nom d’un enfant mineur? : o il sera demandé à fournir des renseignements sur lui-même (voir Partie 1(A) : Données personnelles du parent/tuteur ou du représentant dans le guide d'AVE); o lorsque la demande est soumise, une note sera générée automatiquement dans

### SMGC dans l’écran IMM/CIT > vue Notes de la demande d’AVE :

la colonne Bureau de la note indiquera SMGC-système; et la note comprendra les détails et les coordonnées du parent/gardien ou du représentant qui présente la demande au nom du client. •S'il y a une note d'autorisation qui a été générée automatiquement et créée par SMGC-système avec de tels informations : oaucun IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476 n’est nécessaire afin de l’aider s’il a des questions

## durant le traitement de la demande;

est requis.

##### English II Haut de la page

Client refuse de signer le formulaire d'annulation de représentant

Dans les cas où le client refuse de signer le formulaire d'annulation d'un représentant, les représentants peuvent demander d'être retirés d'un dossier s'ils fournissent :

Un formulaire IMM 5476 dûment rempli, à l'exception de la signature du client; et Une lettre explicative contenant l'information concernant : la raison pour laquelle le représentant veut être retiré du dossier; et la raison pour laquelle le client ne va/ne peut pas signer le formulaire.

000451

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomn

Le formulaire doit avoir été signé il y a 90 jours civils ou moins.

Exception: Vous pouvez accepter un formulaire signé il y a plus de 90 jours civils si vous avez une preuve qu'il a été initialement envoyé dans les 90 jours civils suivant la date de signature. Par exemple :

Le formulaire a été téléversé par le bureau dans l’écran IMM/CIT > vue Correspondance-Reçue ou Docs électroniques; Un ID partie a été créé dans l’écran IMM/CIT > vue Clients et Parties; Une note a été ajoutée par le bureau; Etc.

Après avoir consulté le tableau ci-dessus, vous déterminez que le formulaire est périmé :

- Répondez au requérant en utilisant le texte IMM 5475 ou 5476 - Périmé-Formulaire daté il y a plus de 90 jours; et Ajoutez une note : CSC a reçu formulaire (IMM 5475 ou IMM 5476) incomplet par (courriel OU télécopieur). Requête refusée car le formulaire a été signé (il y a plus de 90 jours civils OU plus de 90 jours civils avant la date de réception de la demande). Courriel envoyé au (client OU représentant) à (entrez adresse de courriel) pour aviser d'envoyer un nouveau formulaire avec une signature mise à jour. INIT-ID
IMM 5475/5476 - Version désuète ou précédente du formulaire

Formulaire IMM 5475 :

Tout nouveau formulaire IMM 5475 reçu doit être la version actuelle de mai 2021 en anglais et en français. Les versions antérieures ne sont plus acceptées.

Formulaire IMM 5476 :

Le formulaire IMM 5476 a été mis à jour en novembre 2021 en anglais et en français.

000452

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomn

À compter du 1 avril 2022, tout IMM 5476 reçu pour une demande nouvellement soumise doit être la version actuelle de novembre 2021. Toute version antérieure du formulaire ne sera plus acceptée à compter de cette date pour toute nouvelle demande présentée.

partie et que toutes les informations correspondent.

The procedure Authorization > Authorization already in the system describes scenarios where no response would be sent to the client including Authorization is already on file in a cancelled application that was transferred to a new application number and certain cases under Authorization is already on file but not properly added. This makes sense when the authorization is validated, as the representative/designated individual expects that they have already been authorized on file. However, there are cases where agents will send /MM 5475 or 5476 – Approved regardless, when following this procedure to confirm the validation was approved (either due to lack of knowledge or habit with other procedures). Sending this response in incorrect and can cause confusion and stress for all parties. The

000486

script /MM 5475 or 5476 – Approved states “The [appointed representative OR designated individual]nowhas authorization to access the file." The word “now” implies that the representative or designated individual did not previously have access. In these cases, the representative/designated individual is never given an explanation as to why it took so long to validate their authorization.

Would it be possible to explicitly make mention of the fact that no response should be sent regarding the approved authorization in these cases? This would be to help better guide agents and to ensure fewer mistakes are being made.

What led you to come up with this request? This came up while briefing Quality colleagues on UOR procedures for the purpose of evaluating emails processed by UOR agents. We noticed that although the procedures mentioned above do not explicitly indicate that a response must be sent regarding the validation when it is approved, certain agents send the script /MM 5475 or 5476 – Approved nonetheless. We would like this point to be clarified so that agents better understand the procedure. It will be made clear that they will loose points if they send this script in situations where it should not have been sent.

Very frequently (i.e., at least once a week) How often do you get emails Frequently (i.e., at least once a month) on this topic? Rarely (i.e., a few times a year) Very rarely (i.e., not more than once per year) Thank you! ### For representatives who are members of a regulatory body:

- Verify if they are in good standing (also available on our website); if online verification service is not available, contact the regulatory body via telephone;
- If the regulatory body's website indicates that the representative has a restrictive permit: follow this procedure. Representatives who are members of the College of Immigration and Citizenship Consultants (CICC) can practice both in Canada and outside Canada; It is possible for an authorized representative to have an address that is outside Canada; in such case, add their address in the note only; and
do not associate their address to the application; Only the lawyer identified in the application can act on behalf of the client; however, information may be divulged to their staff members; please consult this section for more information; For articling students (students-at-law), they are to be identified by name in the note to be added on file along with their supervising lawyer's contact information and membership ID number, and the firm's contact details; Only the Law Society of Ontario admits paralegals as members in good standing:

- if a paralegal is not a member in good standing of a law society or is not a member in good standing of the CICC, then they are not an authorized representative;
- For CIT applications:
all the above information only applies to applications received after June 11, 2015.

Client refuses to sign representative cancellation form

In cases where the client refuses to sign a cancellation form, representatives may ask to be removed from a file if they provide:

A duly filled IMM 5476 form, with the exception that client signature is not necessary; and An explanation letter that contains information about: why the representative wants to be removed from the file; and why the client will not/cannot sign the form.

## IMM 5475/5476 - Outdated or previous version of form

### IMM 5475 form:

Any new IMM 5475 form received must be the current version from May 2021 in English and French. Previous versions are no longer accepted.

### IMM 5476 form:

The IMM 5476 form was updated in November 2021 in English and French.

Starting April 1, 2022, any IMM 5476 received for a newly submitted application must be the current version from November 2021. Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date for any newly submitted application.

Applicant unable to legally act on their own behalf due to disability

If you receive a Web form enquiry where a parent/legal guardian wants to act on behalf of an applicant who has a disability (i.e., they indicate that applicant is unable to read, write, speak, sign documents or make their own decisions), then a Power of Attorney (POA) may be required.

Oif a person is illiterate or cannot make a mark (e.g. an X) for a physical reason, in such cases a thumbprint is acceptable.

### For a sponsorship application:

The spouse or common-law partner can sign the same form; The representative is considered to be acting on behalf of both parties.

### For any other permanent residence application:

If the applicant and the spouse or common-law partner both sign the form: o the representative is considered to be acting on behalf of the applicant identified in section A and (if applicable), their minor dependants; O a separate authorization form is required for the spouse or common-law partner even if they are included in the same application.

# Signature date The form is dated 90 calendar days or less BEFORE the application

reception date: you can accept it even if you don't have proof that it was sent initially with the application. The form is dated anytime AFTER the application reception date: Oit must be signed 90 calendar days ago or less; exception: you can accept a form that was signed more than 90 calendar days ago if you have proof that the form was initially sent within 90 calendar days from the signature date (e.g. the form was uploaded by the office in the IMM/CIT screen > Correspondence-Incoming or e-Docs view, a Party ID was created in the IMM/CIT screen > Client & Parties view, a note was entered by the office, etc.); this includes when the requester resubmits an authorization form with a slight modification.

#### Power of attorney (POA)

IMPoRTANT: The client represented by an attorney is also known as a donor. Until further notice, Immigration Program Guidance (IPG) has confirmed that there are no official guidelines to validate a POA document.

Normally, a POA document should also indicate for which aspects of the donor's life the attorney is authorized for (finance, immigration only, all aspects, etc.).

When validating a POA, please make sure that the following information is provided:

- The attorney's: name; address;
#### phone number and/or email address; and

signature.

#### The donor's:

name; address; phone number and/or email address; date of birth; and signature.

- If no LOB is specified in the POA document, the attorney is authorized on all applications.
Students-at-law

Students-at-law can be representatives even if they are not members in good standing of a Canadian provincial or territorial law society if they meet certain criteria.

### If student-at-law is...Then...

000538

Immigration, Réfugiés Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomnduion

They can be authorized if they are under the supervision of: Compensated

Lawyers and paralegals who are members in good standing of a Canadian provincial or territorial law society; or

# Notaries who are members in good standing of

the Chambre des notaires du Québec.

The member in good standing and/or the student-at-law must provide the IMM 5476 along with a satisfactory explanation, at your discretion, so you can determine with certainty that the student is under the supervision of the member.

Uncompensated They can be authorized if they are under the supervision of consultants, lawyers or Quebec notaries in good standing who do not charge a fee or receive compensation.

The member in good standing and/or the student-at-law must provide the IMM 5476 along with a satisfactory explanation, at your discretion, so you can determine with certainty that the student is under the supervision of the member.

DESCRIPTION The Authorized Paid Representatives Portal was developed to allow authorized paid representatives to complete, pay for and submit applications online on behalf of their clients.

Note: This portal is not connected to the PR Online Application Portal.

Who can use the system? Only authorized paid representatives can use the Authorized Paid Representatives Portal.

# PROCESS

Enrolment request In order to enrol in the portal, the authorized paid representative needs to complete and submit an enrolment request online. To do so, they must first register (or log in) using one of two service providers: GCKey; or

## Sign-In Partner.

### Verifying identity

If the representative is in good standing with their governing body and their identity is ascertained, they will receive an email informing them their enrolment is approved and their account is active.

Once they receive this email, they will be able to login to the portal and begin to submit online

## applications on behalf of clients.

See the After you submit your enrolment request section for further information.

# Applying online

On the portal welcome page, the representative must click on Access Representative Queue, under What would you like to do today? to:

- Apply for:
Visitor visa, study and/or work permit; Express Entry; International Experience Canada (IEC); Interim Federal Health Program (IFHP); Electronic Travel Authorization (ETA);

- Link an existing application to their account.

## Portal for authorized paid representatives

FAQ

1. Can a representative delegate an individual to complete applications on their behalf?
2. How long does a representative have to complete and submit an online application?
3. How can a representative change/review their answers?
4. How many people can be included in a group application?
5. How can representatives contact us if they are experiencing technical difficulties?
6. How can clients modify/remove their representative?
7. Do representatives have to be physically present in Canada, even if the client is overseas?
8. How do representatives update their contact information?
9. What is the time frame to complete the representative enrollment?
10. How can a representative unlock an IRCC Portal for authorized paid representatives account?
1. Can a representative delegate an individual to complete applications on their behalf? No. They cannot provide access to an individual to prepare online applications that will be submitted by them.
2. How long does a representative have to complete and submit an online application? They have 60 days from the date they started it. They can see the days remaining to submit an application in the queue of online applications. If the application is not submitted within 60 days, it is automatically deleted.
3. How can a representative change/review their answers? At any moment, they can change their answers by clicking on Modify my Answers located at the top right corner of the screen of the online tool.
4. How many people can be included in a group application?

The Portal for authorized paid representatives allows representatives to submit group applications (e.g., family members, employees, etc.). A maximum of 10 applicants per group is allowed.

5. How can representatives contact us if they are experiencing technical difficulties?
## They can:

Click on the Report a Technical Issue button located at the top of their account; or Fill out the IRCC Web form.

# 6. How can clients modify/remove their representative?

## Paper applications:

Applications that can be linked: clients can fill out Section C of the Use of a Representative form (IMM 5476) and provide it using the IRCC Web form without linking the application. They will then receive correspondence by mail and/or email; or they may create or sign in to their own online account and link the application to their account to receive online updates; if the application was not previously linked to a representative account, clients should also fill out Section C of the Use of a Representative form (IMM 5476) and provide it using the IRCC Web form. if the application was already linked to the representative account, they will have to upload an IMM 5476 form at the end of the linking process to cancel the representative.

- Applications that cannot be linked:
clients can fill out Section C of the Use of a Representative form (IMM 5476) and provide it using the IRCC Web form. They will then receive correspondence by mail and/or email.

## Online applications:

- Application submitted using a client online account:
clients can login their own online account and link the application to their account to receive online updates. They will have to upload an IMM 5476 form at the end of the linking process to cancel the representative. •Application submitted using the Portal for authorized paid representatives: create or login their own online account and link the application to their account to receive online updates;

if the application was not previously linked to a representative account, clients should also fill out Section C of the Use of a Representative form (IMM 5476) and provide it using the IRCC Web form; if the application was already linked to the representative account, they will have to upload an IMM 5476 at the end of the linking process to either: cancel the representative; or appoint a new representative.

7. Do representatives have to be physically present in Canada, even if the client is overseas?

All representatives must have an address in Canada to enroll in the Portal for authorized paid representatives. However, it is not necessary for them to be physically present in Canada when completing the application.

8. How do representatives update their contact information?

Representatives do not require a business name when they sign up for the Portal. They sign up as individuals who are in good standing with their organizations and meet all of IRCC requirements for enrollment to the Portal.

There is no field in the Portal or in GCMS to update their business name, telephone numbers, addresses, etc. Including their updated information on the new IMM 5476 forms they upload through the Portal or provide via Web form should be sufficient.

9. What is the time frame to complete the representative enrollment?

It should take no longer than 14 calendar days.

10. How can a representative unlock a Portal for authorized paid representatives account?

When a representative answers the Portal for authorized paid representatives security questions incorrectly 8 times (2 attempts for each of the 4 questions), their account becomes locked.

They must then create a new GCKey and re-enroll in the Portal.
